[EVENT: ISR47278]
Date: 2023-11-02
Country: Israel | Location: Khirbet Zarit
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Hezbollah [Political militia]
Actor2: Military Forces of Israel (2022-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: AlManar TV; Times of Israel; Ynet [National-Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
On 2 November 2023, Hezbollah forces fired guided missiles, artillery shells, and 'direct weapons' at Khirbet Zarit (Akko, HaZafon). They claimed to have achieved direct hits. No casualties. They claimed to have simultaneously attacked 19 IDF military sites on this day. The IDF claimed that some of the rockets in Hezbollah's large scale barrage were intercepted and others fell in undisclosed open areas.

---

DESCRIPTION:
On 9 November 2023, dozens of Israeli Arabs, some of whom are affiliated with Hadash, protested outside of a police station in Tel Aviv city against the Israel-Hamas War and against the recent arrest of former Arab MKs for planning an anti-war protest in Nazareth. Police forces detained about 15 protesters outside of the police station. Earlier in the day, police forces detained at least four former MKs from the Hadash and Balad parties who were planning an anti-war protest in Nazareth.

Five people were injured while running to bomb shelters due to the barrage toward central Israel.

[EVENT: ISR38211]
Date: 2023-11-18
Country: Israel | Location: Tel Aviv
Type: Protests > Protest with intervention | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Actor2: Rioters (Israel) [Rioters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Haaretz; Times of Israel; Ynet [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 18 November 2023, hundreds of Israeli Arabs and Jews protested in Tel Aviv city, calling for an immediate ceasefire and an end to the war. Protesters also called for an 'all-for-all' hostage deal, in which all Israeli hostages are released in exchange for 'Palestinian political prisoners unlawfully held in Israel.' Hadash organized the protest. A Hadash MK took part in the protest, in addition to former MKs from Labor and the Joint List. Dozens of opponents to the anti-war protest, who did not appear to be holding a counter-protest, threw bottles and other objects at the protesters. Heated confrontations took place between the rioters and some of the protesters. Police forces physically separated the two sides and detained one protester.

[EVENT: ISR38240]

Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Israel (2022-) Prison Guards [State forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Palestine) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: 972; Ma'an News Agency; Newpress; PLO Negotiations Affairs Department; Quds News Network [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 18 November 2023, a Palestinian detainee died in Ktzi'ot Prison in the Negev Desert area (Beer Sheva, HaDarom) after he was beaten by Israeli prison guards. The deceased had been in prison since 2005, serving a 25-year sentence. Fatah movement and Palestinian National and Islamic Forces announced a general strike in Qalqilya province in protest against his assassination. This is the 6th case of assassination allegations concerning Palestinian prisoners in Israeli prisons since 7 October 2023. An independent source corroborated Palestinian allegations that the detainee died due to a severe beating by prison guards.

[EVENT: ISR38608]
Date: 2023-12-24
Country: Israel | Location: Margaliyyot
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Hezbollah [Political militia]
Actor2: Military Forces of Israel (2022-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Hezbollah War Media; Ynet [New media-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 24 December 2023, Hezbollah forces fired a number of rockets from Lebanon toward Margaliyyot (Zefat, HaZafon). Two anti-tank missiles fell in Margaliyyot, causing property damage, while at least three rockets fell in open areas outside of the town. Hezbollah claimed to have targeted Israeli soldiers in Hunin Castle. Israeli military forces struck the source of the launches in Lebanon with tank and artillery fire.

---

[EVENT: ISR38540]
Date: 2023-12-26
Country: Israel | Location: Iqrit
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Hezbollah [Political militia]
Actor2: Military Forces of Israel (2022-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Arutz Sheva; Haaretz; Israel Hayom; Ma'ariv; N12; Times of Israel; Ynet [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 26 December 2023, Hezbollah forces launched two anti-tank missiles from Lebanon at Iqrit (Akko, HaZafon). The first missile hit a church in Iqrit, injuring one civilian. When Israeli military forces were responding to the direct hit, Hezbollah forces fired a second missile, injuring nine Israeli soldiers. In response, Israeli military forces struck the source of the missiles.
